## Arcade Memories

Path: `MUSICIANS/C/Chiummo_Gaetano/Arcade_Memories_3SID.sid`

Author: Gaetano Chiummo · Release/year: 2014 Samar Productions

PSID; BASIC flag clear; 1 subtunes. Complete-file MD5: `dc5c173e6895b140500b107b94d8a3c0`; SHA-256: `825c59895f7e034c14fd53ac6e318f3197974753e547fcf3c987016a0b2da74e`.

| Subtune | Seconds | Evidence | Reference RMS; writes | Neither | K-only | B-only |
|---:|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2 | pcm-difference-with-similar-write-sequence | 5.822e-2; 3745 | ΔRMS 1.180e-2; writes 3745; prefix 3745; drift 13; derived | identical PCM; writes 3745; prefix 3745; drift 0; derived | ΔRMS 1.180e-2; writes 3745; prefix 3745; drift 13 |

STIL (full available entry and applicable directory notes):

> Directory note /MUSICIANS/C/Chiummo_Gaetano/
> COMMENT: Gaetano Chiummo's own comments are denoted (GC).
>
> COMMENT: "The inspiration for the tune came from the arcade game 'Zero Wing', especially
>          that noisy rhythm pattern! The song is very short, and features a very fast solo
>          at the end which is one of my most favorite things in the whole collection. In
>          the last part there's a little joke: the tune goes 'lo-fi' (only three
>          rectangular waveforms are used), and some sound effects play along; this should
>          give the idea of someone actually playing a game! If you listen carefully, you
>          can figure out what is happening: the player shoots some alien robots, gets some
>          power-ups and eventually gets hit! I had a lot of fun composing this part, as I
>          had to design the sound effects too!" (GC)

> Directory note /MUSICIANS/C/Chiummo_Gaetano/
> COMMENT: Gaetano Chiummo's own comments are denoted (GC).
>
> COMMENT: "History of Headaches is another piece I like very much. This tune was composed
>          for a Tape Loader Compo, and I wanted it to sound like Galway's Ocean Loaders
>          tunes, without using any drums and trying to have the bass playing the rhythmical
>          part. I also didn't want to 'multiplex' too much, and leave each note play
>          without being stopped abruptly by the next one. There's also an 'echo' effect on
>          the lead instrument, which gives more depth to the sound." (GC)

## New Adventure

Path: `MUSICIANS/C/Chiummo_Gaetano/New_Adventure_3SID.sid`

Author: Gaetano Chiummo · Release/year: 2014 Samar Productions

PSID; BASIC flag clear; 1 subtunes. Complete-file MD5: `91bdbb29baf5734bd8d460091cfe7c4b`; SHA-256: `5a4b109699a0246f835389e031b40ecfad14f2006578585f6f0cd381c21482dc`.

| Subtune | Seconds | Evidence | Reference RMS; writes | Neither | K-only | B-only |
|---:|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2 | pcm-difference-with-similar-write-sequence | 4.915e-2; 3993 | ΔRMS 1.784e-2; writes 3993; prefix 3993; drift 13; derived | identical PCM; writes 3993; prefix 3993; drift 0; derived | ΔRMS 1.784e-2; writes 3993; prefix 3993; drift 13 |

STIL (full available entry and applicable directory notes):

> Directory note /MUSICIANS/C/Chiummo_Gaetano/
> COMMENT: Gaetano Chiummo's own comments are denoted (GC).
>
> COMMENT: "I composed the main harmony on guitar, and then finished the tune and arranged
>          it on Psycle, trying to figure out how I could use the three SIDs at their best.
>          The most obvious thing to do was to dedicate one SID to the chords (this was a
>          bit limiting, as I could only use three notes chords), another SID to the lead
>          instrument and the bass, and another one to the drums. As for the lead
>          instrument, I could use two tracks: one for the main instrument, and the other
>          one for a counter melody or for some special effects, such as the 'sync'. As for
>          the drums, the snare and the hi-hat used two voices both, while the bass drum
>          only played on one track: this made the arrangement a bit difficult, as I didn't
>          want to make any instrument sound 'stopped'." (GC)
